- **pay-or-refuse**: Pay an x402 (HTTP 402 Payment Required) resource only when vet402's evidence says the seller delivers, and refuse before any signature exists otherwise. Covers the MCP tool pay_if_trusted (@vet402/mcp-server), the SDK call payOrRefuse (@vet402/sdk), every refuse reason code and what to do on each, and how to make The Graph's x402 Base subgraph your own evidence source so you do not have to trust vet402's ledger.
